I’m not sure I’d call it guilt exactly. I mostly just feel kind of uncomfortable with myself afterward, like, “Why did I do that?” But I try not to make a huge deal out of it. Is that what you mean?\n\nDoctor: How often do these episodes of eating a lot more than you meant to happen?\n\nPatient: Most weeks it’s… at least one night, I think. I don’t want to make it sound worse than it is, but it’s been happening pretty regularly for a few months. And sometimes after, I’ll try to kind of “fix” it—like skip a meal, work out extra, or… sometimes make myself throw up or take something. I know that probably doesn’t sound great.\n\nDoctor: How often do you make yourself throw up or take something after one of these eating episodes?\n\nPatient: Um… not every time. I don’t want you to think I’m doing it constantly, but… sometimes after I eat too much, I’ll go to the bathroom and make myself throw up. And other times I’ll take laxatives. It’s mostly because I get scared about what all that food is going to do to me, if that makes sense.\n\nDoctor: How many times a week do you make yourself throw up or take laxatives after eating?\n\nPatient: I guess… maybe a couple times a week? Not every time I overeat, but sometimes after I eat too much I’ll go make myself throw up, or I’ll take laxatives. I know that probably sounds bad… I just get really scared about what the food is going to do to me.\n\nDoctor: Do you try to make yourself throw up or take laxatives every time you overeat?\n\nPatient: No… not every time. I mean, sometimes I can just kind of sit with it, or try to move on. But yeah, there are times after I eat too much where I’ll go to the bathroom and make myself throw up, or I’ll take laxatives. I know it’s not a good way to handle it… I just get really scared about what the food is going to do to me.\n\nDoctor: On average, how many times a week do you eat a lot more than you meant to without then making yourself throw up, taking laxatives, or trying to compensate in some other way?\n\nPatient: Um… I guess that happens too. Like after a long shift, or if I’ve been studying and I’m tired, I’ll mean to just grab something quick, and then it turns into a full fast-food meal, snacks, and then stuff from the fridge when I get home… all kind of in one sitting.\n\nI don’t always do anything after, though. Maybe… a few times a week? I’m not sure if that’s the kind of number you mean.\n\nDoctor: Do you think your weight is normal, too high, or too low?\n\nPatient: I mean… I know on paper I’m probably not, like, extremely one way or the other. But to me it feels too high, I guess.\n\nLike I’ll look in the mirror before heading to the shop, and if my stomach or chest looks soft, it just… it makes me feel sloppy, like in general, not just how I look. I know that probably sounds kind of dramatic.\n\nDoctor: How many times a week do you eat a lot more than you meant to without doing anything to try to compensate for it?\n\nPatient: I think… maybe around three times a week? It’s not like I’m trying to do it on purpose, but I’ll get off a long shift or finish studying and I’ll end up eating way more than I meant to—like a whole fast-food meal, then snacks, and whatever’s in the fridge—just kind of all in one sitting. And those times I don’t always do anything after. Does that sound like the kind of thing you’re asking?\n\nDoctor: How often do you make yourself throw up after eating?\n\nPatient: Um… not after every time, and I’m not proud of it. But sometimes when I feel like I ate too much, I’ll go to the bathroom and make myself throw up because I get scared about what the food is going to do to me.\n\nI don’t know the exact number… maybe a couple times in a week, depending on how bad things are. It feels a little embarrassing to say, but… I figured I should probably ask someone about it.\n\nDoctor: Do you usually feel guilty after eating that much?\n\nPatient: I mean… maybe a little? Not every time I overeat, but sometimes after I eat too much I’ll go make myself throw up, or I’ll take laxatives. And sometimes after, I’ll try to kind of “fix” it—like skip a meal, work out extra, or… sometimes make myself throw up or take something. I know that probably doesn’t sound great.\n\nDoctor: How often do you make yourself throw up or take something after one of these eating episodes?\n\nPatient: Um… not every time. I don’t want you to think I’m doing it constantly, but… sometimes after I eat too much, I’ll go to the bathroom and make myself throw up. And other times I’ll take laxatives. It’s mostly because I get scared about what all that food is going to do to me, if that makes sense.\n\nDoctor: How many times a week do you make yourself throw up or take laxatives after eating?\n\nPatient: I guess… maybe a couple times a week? Not every time I overeat, but sometimes after I eat too much I’ll go make myself throw up, or I’ll take laxatives. I know that probably sounds bad… I just get really scared about what the food is going to do to me.\n\nDoctor: Do you try to make yourself throw up or take laxatives every time you overeat?\n\nPatient: No… not every time. I mean, sometimes I can just kind of sit with it, or try to move on. But yeah, there are times after I eat too much where I’ll go to the bathroom and make myself throw up, or I’ll take laxatives. I know it’s not a good way to handle it… I just get really scared about what the food is going to do to me.\n\nDoctor: On average, how many times a week do you eat a lot more than you meant to without then making yourself throw up, taking laxatives, or trying to compensate in some other way?\n\nPatient: Um… I guess that happens too. Like after a long shift, or if I’ve been studying and I’m tired, I’ll mean to just grab something quick, and then it turns into a full fast-food meal, snacks, and then stuff from the fridge when I get home… all kind of in one sitting.\n\nI don’t always do anything after, though. Maybe… a few times a week? I’m not sure if that’s the kind of number you mean.\n\nDoctor: Do you think your weight is normal, too high, or too low?\n\nPatient: I mean… I know on paper I’m probably not, like, extremely one way or the other. But to me it feels too high, I guess.\n\nLike I’ll look in the mirror before heading to the shop, and if my stomach or chest looks soft, it just… it makes me feel sloppy, like in general, not just how I look. I know that probably sounds kind of dramatic.\n\nDoctor: How many times a week do you eat a lot more than you meant to without doing anything to try to compensate for it?\n\nPatient: I think… maybe around three times a week? It’s not like I’m trying to do it on purpose, but I’ll get off a long shift or finish studying and I’ll end up eating way more than I meant to—like a whole fast-food meal, then snacks, and whatever’s in the fridge—just kind of all in one sitting. But sometimes when I feel like I ate too much, I’ll go to the bathroom and make myself throw up because I get scared about what the food is going to do to me.\n\nI don’t know the exact number… maybe a couple times in a week, depending on how bad things are.
